# Gods recovery ledger

Append-only, one line per recovered region or escalation, newest last.  The
grinder's only per-region record; `STATUS.md` is updated once per milestone.

Line format (pipe-separated, one line):

    date | entry / arms | shape | semantics | tests | cold run (node, frames, artifacts dir, status) | hits, fallbacks | commit or blocker

`hits` and `fallbacks` are the candidate worker's `candidate_stats` from the
named cold run's `comparison.json`.  A region whose hits are zero on the
recorded histories is `NOT_EXERCISED` and does not belong here.

Census over all eight recordings (`--classifier entry --max-classes 400`, `artifacts/gods/evidence/census-008222-entry[-<node>]`, 271 real path classes, no overflow) found sub-pass 2's own item DOES have ITEM_CONTACT_WORD 1 on 96 of those fixtures -- the latch-test code itself is witnessed and admitted (the latch is simply always still clear when it runs), only the latch actually being set (sub-pass 1's own arm) and a found match being gated by it stay declined, alongside a sub-pass 2/3 whose own ITEM_CONTACT_WORD is negative ('skip-negative', unwitnessed on all 271 fixtures).  Every instruction's own cost is data-independent (confirmed against the tracer on every distinct instruction the routine executes), so the boundary costs it one instruction-block at a time rather than per fixed path, generalizing past the retained fixtures the way `proximity_plan` does.  Two register subtleties only exposed by a fixture whose caller entry register upper halves were nonzero (`008222-entry-p34`, `cfd02803e937`): D0/D3/D5 exit with upper half always zero (HEAD's own unconditional `moveq`s, not the caller's own entry value, the way every OTHER register this planner touches preserves it); D4/D6 do preserve the caller's own upper half (no `moveq` of their own touches them).  The three `bsr` calls into `00837E` per sub-pass push a transient return address at (entry `a7` − 4) that `00837E`'s own `rts` pops straight back before the next one runs -- real stack residue from the LAST call made, not dead scratch, modelled as one more write | a leaf (no save/restore frame at all, hazard_tick's own shape) with three independent sub-passes and a shared bounded-retry helper, costed one instruction-block at a time (`game.pickups._contact_probe`/`_contact_subpass`/`contact_search`; Getting the COMPOSITION right (the standalone `trail_check_plan` gate needed none of this) surfaced three real facts about the raiser's own preamble no prior session's kind-3-only composition had needed, each caught by `factcheck check`'s own byte-exact residue comparison, none guessed: (1) the raiser's own dispatch computes `d0 = (status - 1) * 4` (the EVENT_TABLE offset, not the raw status word) and 0044C0 never touches d0 again before its own `movem.l d0-d7,-(a7)`, so that value -- 0x14 for kind 6 -- is what its own transient frame residue must carry, not the status itself; (2) the raiser's OWN 00773A head sets D1 to the tile row (`(y & TILE_Y_MASK) << 3`, exposed as `game.player.tile_row`) unconditionally, before any position is even tested -- NOT the tail activation's own entry D1 -- so a kind-6 raise's own register-frame push needs that value threaded in, carried the same way A4 already was, across however many raises one activation makes; (3) the raiser's own dispatch (`movea.l (a1,d0.w),a1`) leaves A1 = the handler's own address (`0x0044C0`) before the `jsr`, and since 0044C0 never touches A1 itself (only absolute addressing throughout) that value rides through to the tail's own final exit untouched -- 00462C, by contrast, immediately overwrites A1 with the trigger table base, which is why this never surfaced during the kind-3-only composition.
